EUR is the base currency. Rates are stored as the ECB's published text, and all\narithmetic uses `decimal`, never floating point.\n\n## Project Layout\n\n| Path | Purpose |\n|------|---------|\n| `pyproject.toml`, `uv.lock` | Python 3.11+ project; dependencies fastapi, uvicorn, httpx2, pytest; committed lockfile. |\n| `.venv` | Environment created by `uv venv`; launchers in `bin/` and `fxrates` activate it. |\n| `fxrates` | Executable at the application root; a POSIX sh launcher that calls `cli.main`. |\n| `bin/test.sh` | Runs `pytest -q` offline. |\n| `fxrates_app/` | Application package: `config`, `ecb`, `fetch`, `rates`, `history`, `convert`, `commands`. |\n| `cli/` | Command line foundation: `main`, dispatcher, usage validation, `testing.cli_runner`. |\n| `app_server/` | Web foundation: `create_app`, module-level `app`, `testing.web_client`. |\n| `persistence/` | Generated by Drydock from `DATABASE.md`; never hand-written. |\n| `tests/` | pytest suite with `conftest.py`, written beside the code. |\n| `sources/` | Read-only scoring assets; never read by application code. |\n\n## Module Ownership\n\n| Boundary | Owning module | Allowed low-level access |\n|----------|---------------|--------------------------|\n| Persistence | `persistence` (generated) | The only module that imports a database driver. |\n| Configuration | `fxrates_app.config` | Reads `DATABASE_URL`, `DATABASE_PATH`; opens the store via `persistence.open_database`. |\n| ECB parsing | `fxrates_app.ecb` | Pure functions over CSV text; no I/O to the store. |\n| Network | `fxrates_app.fetch` | `download_archive(url)` is the only network call and the test seam. |\n| Rate queries | `fxrates_app.rates`, `history`, `convert` | Call `db.rates.<operation>(...)` only. |\n| Command line | `cli` | Dispatch, usage validation, exit codes; diagnostics to standard error. |\n| HTTP | `app_server` | Application factory and shared router registry. |\n\nRoutes and commands call services; services call `db.rates.<operation>(...)`. SQL, table names, and\ncolumn names never cross the persistence boundary. Application code never imports `sqlite3` or any\nother driver.\n\n## Technology Stack\n\n| Technology | Where it applies |\n|------------|------------------|\n| Python 3.11+ | Whole application. |\n| FastAPI, uvicorn | JSON API served on `127.0.0.1`, default port 8000. |\n| SQLite | The `rates` table, reached through the shipped persistence library. |\n| CLI | The `fxrates` executable. |\n| pytest, FastAPI `TestClient` | Test suite behind `bin/test.sh`. |\n| ECB reference rates | Input: `eurofxref-hist.csv`, downloaded as `eurofxref-hist.zip`. |\n| POSIX sh | `fxrates` launcher and `bin/test.sh`. |\n\n## Technical Decisions\n\n- Exit codes: `0` success, `1` operational failure, `2` usage error, `3` rejected file.\n- Failing commands write nothing to standard output and one diagnostic line to standard error.\n- Database location: `DATABASE_URL` (optional `sqlite://` prefix), then `DATABASE_PATH`, then `data/app.db`.\n- Tests never open a network connection; `fetch` tests replace `fxrates_app.fetch.download_archive`.\n- Diagnostics go to standard error and are never compared.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC arch-project-manifest ===\nIntent: The project root declares a Python requirement and the stack dependencies, commits a lockfile, and has a pytest conftest.\nRequires: executable=python3; This story proves only the interface.\n\n## Interface\n\n```python\n# cli/__init__.py\ndef main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int: ...\ndef register_command(name: str, handler: Callable[[list[str]], int]) -> None: ...\n\n# cli/testing.py\ndef cli_runner(*args: str) -> tuple[int, str, str]: ...\n```\n\n- `main` dispatches `argv[0]` to the registered handler, passing the remaining arguments, and\n  returns the handler's integer exit code. Diagnostics go to standard error.\n- `register_command` is called by command stories at import time; registering a name again replaces\n  the earlier handler.\n- `cli_runner` calls `main` in-process and returns `(exit_code, stdout, stderr)`.\n- `fxrates` is a POSIX sh script at the application root with the executable bit set. It activates\n  `.venv` when present, runs `cli.main` with its arguments, and exits with the returned code.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- None — persistence is owned by DATABASE.md.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC cli-main-returns-int ===\nIntent: main returns an integer exit code and cli_runner reports it with captured streams.\nRequires: executable=python3; there is no authentication because only public ECB reference rates are served.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-CliUsageErrors.md ===\n# FEATURE: CLI Usage Errors\n\n| Field | Value |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261027 V1 |\n| Description | Argument validation that reports usage errors with exit 2, empty stdout, and one stderr line. |\n| Depends On | FEATURE-CliFoundation.md |\n| Provides | fxrates usage validation |\n| Consumes | cli.main |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nReject malformed invocations before any handler, database, or socket is touched.\n\n## Behavior\n\n`cli/usage.py` provides `UsageError` and `validate(argv: list[str]) -> None`; `cli.main` calls it\nbefore dispatch and, on `UsageError`, writes one line to standard error, nothing to standard output,\nand returns `2`.\n\n| Command | Arguments |\n|---------|-----------|\n| `init` | none |\n| `load` | exactly one (the CSV path) |\n| `fetch` | none |\n| `serve` | optional `--port <n>`; `n` an integer from 1 to 65535 |\n\n- No command, or a command in neither this table nor the handler registry, is a usage error.\n- A wrong argument count, a `--port` without a value, a non-integer `--port`, and a `--port`\n  outside 1 to 65535 are usage errors.\n- A command registered but absent from the table is not arity-checked.\n- A lone `--help` after a known command is passed through to its handler.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- None — persistence is owned by DATABASE.md.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC usage-rejected-invocations ===\nIntent: Each malformed invocation exits 2 with empty stdout and exactly one stderr line.\nRequires: executable=python3; BEGIN ARTIFACT ARCHITECTURE.md ===\n# ARCHITECTURE: FxRates\n\n| Field | Value |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261027 V1 |\n| Description | Modules, boundaries, and technical decisions for the fxrates command line and JSON API over SQLite. |\n| Depends On | DATABASE.md |\n| Provides | fxrates_app package, pytest runner |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Overview\n\nFxRates keeps the ECB euro reference rate history in one SQLite table and serves it two ways: the\n`fxrates` executable (`init`, `load`, `fetch`, `serve`) and a FastAPI JSON API bound to\n`127.0.0.1`. EUR is the base currency. Rates are stored as the ECB's published text, and all\narithmetic uses `decimal`, never floating point.\n\n## Project Layout\n\n| Path | Purpose |\n|------|---------|\n| `pyproject.toml`, `uv.lock` | Python 3.11+ project; dependencies fastapi, uvicorn, httpx2, pytest; committed lockfile. |\n| `.venv` | Environment created by `uv venv`; launchers in `bin/` and `fxrates` activate it. |\n| `fxrates` | Executable at the application root; a POSIX sh launcher that calls `cli.main`. |\n| `bin/test.sh` | Runs `pytest -q` offline. |\n| `fxrates_app/` | Application package: `config`, `ecb`, `fetch`, `rates`, `history`, `convert`, `commands`. |\n| `cli/` | Command line foundation: `main`, dispatcher, usage validation, `testing.cli_runner`. |\n| `app_server/` | Web foundation: `create_app`, module-level `app`, `testing.web_client`. |\n| `persistence/` | Generated by Drydock from `DATABASE.md`; never hand-written. |\n| `tests/` | pytest suite with `conftest.py`, written beside the code. |\n| `sources/` | Read-only scoring assets; never read by application code. |\n\n## Module Ownership\n\n| Boundary | Owning module | Allowed low-level access |\n|----------|---------------|--------------------------|\n| Persistence | `persistence` (generated) | The only module that imports a database driver. |\n| Configuration | `fxrates_app.config` | Reads `DATABASE_URL`, `DATABASE_PATH`; opens the store via `persistence.open_database`. |\n| ECB parsing | `fxrates_app.ecb` | Pure functions over CSV text; no I/O to the store. |\n| Network | `fxrates_app.fetch` | `download_archive(url)` is the only network call and the test seam. |\n| Rate queries | `fxrates_app.rates`, `history`, `convert` | Call `db.rates.<operation>(...)` only. |\n| Command line | `cli` | Dispatch, usage validation, exit codes; diagnostics to standard error. |\n| HTTP | `app_server` | Application factory and shared router registry. |\n\nRoutes and commands call services; services call `db.rates.<operation>(...)`. SQL, table names, and\ncolumn names never cross the persistence boundary. Application code never imports `sqlite3` or any\nother driver.\n\n## Technology Stack\n\n| Technology | Where it applies |\n|------------|------------------|\n| Python 3.11+ | Whole application. |\n| FastAPI, uvicorn | JSON API served on `127.0.0.1`, default port 8000. |\n| SQLite | The `rates` table, reached through the shipped persistence library. |\n| CLI | The `fxrates` executable. |\n| pytest, FastAPI `TestClient` | Test suite behind `bin/test.sh`. |\n| ECB reference rates | Input: `eurofxref-hist.csv`, downloaded as `eurofxref-hist.zip`. |\n| POSIX sh | `fxrates` launcher and `bin/test.sh`. |\n\n## Technical Decisions\n\n- Exit codes: `0` success, `1` operational failure, `2` usage error, `3` rejected file.\n- Failing commands write nothing to standard output and one diagnostic line to standard error.\n- Database location: `DATABASE_URL` (optional `sqlite://` prefix), then `DATABASE_PATH`, then `data/app.db`.\n- Tests never open a network connection; `fetch` tests replace `fxrates_app.fetch.download_archive`.\n- Diagnostics go to standard error and are never compared.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC arch-project-manifest ===\nIntent: The project root declares a Python requirement and the stack dependencies, commits a lockfile, and has a pytest conftest.\nRequires: executable=python3; This story proves only the interface.\n\n## Interface\n\n```python\n# cli/__init__.py\ndef main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int: ...\ndef register_command(name: str, handler: Callable[[list[str]], int]) -> None: ...\n\n# cli/testing.py\ndef cli_runner(*args: str) -> tuple[int, str, str]: ...\n```\n\n- `main` dispatches `argv[0]` to the registered handler, passing the remaining arguments, and\n  returns the handler's integer exit code. Diagnostics go to standard error.\n- `register_command` is called by command stories at import time; registering a name again replaces\n  the earlier handler.\n- `cli_runner` calls `main` in-process and returns `(exit_code, stdout, stderr)`.\n- `fxrates` is a POSIX sh script at the application root with the executable bit set. It activates\n  `.venv` when present, runs `cli.main` with its arguments, and exits with the returned code.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- None — persistence is owned by DATABASE.md.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC cli-main-returns-int ===\nIntent: main returns an integer exit code and cli_runner reports it with captured streams.\nRequires: executable=python3; `cli.main` calls it\nbefore dispatch and, on `UsageError`, writes one line to standard error, nothing to standard output,\nand returns `2`.\n\n| Command | Arguments |\n|---------|-----------|\n| `init` | none |\n| `load` | exactly one (the CSV path) |\n| `fetch` | none |\n| `serve` | optional `--port <n>`; `n` an integer from 1 to 65535 |\n\n- No command, or a command in neither this table nor the handler registry, is a usage error.\n- A wrong argument count, a `--port` without a value, a non-integer `--port`, and a `--port`\n  outside 1 to 65535 are usage errors.\n- A command registered but absent from the table is not arity-checked.\n- A lone `--help` after a known command is passed through to its handler.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- None — persistence is owned by DATABASE.md.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC usage-rejected-invocations ===\nIntent: Each malformed invocation exits 2 with empty stdout and exactly one stderr line.\nRequires: executable=python3;
